## mutex_rwlock_text_value_nulled_by_pure_std_backend_2026-07-28 — Mutex/RwLock silently null non-int/float/bool values (text) under the default PureStd concurrent backend

- Classification: `valid`, `untested`, `known`, structural change `no`; action `fixed_pending_verification`.
- Inspection: `source_reviewed`; review state: `accepted`.
- Evidence refs: `input/open_bugs_04.tsv bug_id=mutex_rwlock_text_value_nulled_by_pure_std_backend_2026-07-28`; [c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0:src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/concurrent_providers/std_impl.rs:608](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0/src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/concurrent_providers/std_impl.rs#L608); [c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0:src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs:570](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0/src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs#L570); [c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0:src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs:666](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0/src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs#L666); [c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0:src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs:867](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/c0b3ad911f4cb00c0f3550b05baa8d06347d17d0/src/compiler_rust/compiler/src/interpreter_extern/atomic.rs#L867)
- Review rationale: Traced PureStd lock provider through interpreter externs rather than stopping at LockProvider trait. Conversion no longer maps text to NIL: shared runtime bridge handles heap values, protected slots retain originals, and nonrepresentable nonnil values receive distinct tokens. Mutex and rwlock creation/use share that boundary. This directly repairs the historical nulling mechanism locally; current runtime coverage remains untested.
- Review corrections: Seed-owned does not mean uncertain or invalid. Original conversion defect is evidenced by the actual replaced NIL branch and current protected-value implementation.

- Review rationale: Disassembly in the report establishes a missing self argument for immutable fn instance methods. - Review corrections: structural_change: no -> yes; recommended_action: fix_candidate -> fixed_pending_verification. Correct structural no to yes because the remedy changes ABI ownership across HIR, MIR and callers. Source remediation exists; the report bridge is not itself the fix.

- Review rationale: Original report says ExistsCheck's is_some bool was used as a struct address. Current lowering keeps the condition separate, unwraps payload into some_value, copies it into a shared result slot, emits nil on absence, propagates struct/class owner metadata and returns result_local. This is the requested local source remedy. The inspected optional_struct_field fixture uses unwrap rather than the exact .?.field shape, so it does not establish the specific regression; a faithful imported optional getter/field native check is still required.
- Review corrections: Full report itself says OPEN rather than the inventory description's SOURCE FIXED claim; the source independently supports pending verification. Nearby optional unwrap fixture is not exact ExistsCheck coverage.
